function StatePar(self);
  if (self.Allocate = 'C')
    if ((self.ST=0) and (self.ERR=0) )
      self.selframe(1);
    end;
    if ((self.ST=1) and (self.ERR=0) )
      self.selframe(2);
    end;
    if ((self.ERR>0) )
      self.selframe(3);
    end;
  end;
endf;

  local ParL, cmd, PStat, x, tm; //  
 MPK

  local hand, water_v, hand_str, hand_w_str;
  kod_arr := [[160, 165, 176, 181, 512],         //
  [144, 149, 176, 181, 256],         //
  [5, 133, 149, 165, 181],       //
  [2, 3],                                           //
  [1, 128, 133]];                           //
  cmd := 'ParL' + STR( self.Line, 1 );
  ParL := &&cmd;
  cmd := 'PStat' + STR( self.Line, 1 );
  PStat := &&cmd;
  cmd := 'hand_on';
  hand := &&cmd;
  hand_str:=' ';
  hand_w_str:=' ';
  //      
  if ( self.Oper <> 0 )
    
    if ( ( Int( PStat.WASH_START ) = 0 ) )
      prj.SetWashStart(PStat,now);
    end;
    
    if ( self.step_no = 0 )
      // 
      // 
      self.step_no := self.step_no + 1;
      self.Prev_oper := self.Oper;
      self.start_step := NOW;
    else
      
      tm:=prj.GetWashStart(Pstat);
      
      if (self.Line=1) hand_w_str:=hand.str_w_l1;  end;
      if (self.Line=2) hand_w_str:=hand.str_w_l2;  end;//add new line
      if (self.Line=3) hand_w_str:=hand.str_w_l3;  end;
      if (self.Line=4) hand_w_str:=hand.str_w_l4;  end;
      if (self.Line=5) hand_w_str:=hand.str_w_l5;  end;
      if (self.Line=6) hand_w_str:=hand.str_w_l6;  end;
      
      {if (self.start_step < Pstat.start_time)
        self.start_step := Pstat.start_time;
      end;}
      
      {x:=null;
      x:=VarArrayCreate([0, 12], varVariant);
      x[0]  := 'table';
      x[1]  := 'reports.msa_steps';
      x[2]  := str(self.mca, 1) +' '+ str(self.line, 1) +' '+ str( tm, 1 );          //'0'+str(self.mca,1)+'0'+str(self.line,1)+date;
      x[3]  := self.step_no;           //
      x[4]  := self.Prev_oper;         //
      x[5]  := 'datetype';
      x[6]  := self.start_step;
      x[7]  := 'datetype';
      x[8]  := NOW;
      x[9]  := ParL.P06_1;                //
      x[10] := ParL.P08_1;                //
      if (LENGTH(PStat.step_errors)>=100)
        x[11] := LEFT(PStat.step_errors, 96) + '...';
      else
        x[11] := PStat.step_errors;
      end;
      x[12] := hand_w_str;
      self.WriteToLog(11, x);
      x:=null;}
      
      ParL.P06Stop := 1;
      ParL.P08Stop := 1;
      ParL.P06_1 := 0;
      ParL.P08_1 := 0;
      
      self.Prev_oper := self.Oper;
      self.step_no := self.step_no + 1;
      self.start_step := NOW;
      PStat.step_errors:='';
      
      if (self.Line=1) hand.str_w_l1:=' ';  end;
      if (self.Line=2) hand.str_w_l2:=' ';  end;//add new line
      if (self.Line=3) hand.str_w_l3:=' ';  end;
      if (self.Line=4) hand.str_w_l4:=' ';  end;
      if (self.Line=5) hand.str_w_l5:=' ';  end;
      if (self.Line=6) hand.str_w_l6:=' ';  end;
           
      if ((self.Oper = 555) and (ParL.P11 <> 0))
        
        if (self.line = 1)
          water_v:=hand.res_v100;
        end;
        if (self.line = 2) //add new line
          water_v:=hand.res_v200;
        end;
        if (self.line = 3)
          water_v:=hand.res_v300;
        end;
        if (self.line = 4)

if (self.allocate='C')
  if (self.state = -1000)
    self.Repair := 1;
  else
    self.Repair := 0;
  end; 
  self.State_str := prj.GetStateStr(self.State);
  ref:= self.R('S1');
  if (self.priority>1)
    
    if (self.State<-1)
      s:=alltrim(str(-1*self.State));
      snl := alltrim(str(self.line));
      ref.PlayOnce('l'+snl, '_'+s);
    else
      if (self.State=-1)
        ref.PlayOnce('stop');
      else
        if (self.State=0)
          ref.PlayOnce('finish');
        end;
      end;
    end;
    
    
  end;
end;

State

  if (self.State=1)
    self.setframes(0);
    self.selframe(2);
  end;
  if (self.State=0)
    self.setframes(0);
    self.selframe(1);
  end;

    self.setframes(0);
    self.selframe(3);
  end;

    self.setframes(0, 6);
    self.selframe(1);
  end;
  if (self.State=11)
    self.setframes(0, 6);
    self.selframe(2);
  end;
  if (self.State=-2)
    self.setframes(0, 7);
    self.selframe(8);
  end;
  if (self.State=-3)
    self.setframes(0);
    self.selframe(3);
  end;             
  if (self.State=21)
    self.setframes(4,0);
    self.selframe(2);
  end;
  if (self.State=20)
    self.setframes(4,0);
    self.selframe(1);
  end;
  {if (self.State=-11)
    self.setframes(4,0);
    self.selframe(3); 
  end;                }
  if (self.State=30)
    self.setframes(4, 0, 6);
    self.selframe(1);
  end;
  if (self.State=31)
    self.setframes(4, 0, 6);
    self.selframe(2);
  end;

  end;

  end;             
end;

v100
v100
if (self.allocate='S')
  local cmd, hand;
  cmd := 'hand_on';
  hand := &&cmd;
  if (self.v100=1)   //
    hand.start_v100:=NOW;
  end;
  if ((self.v100=0) and (hand.start_v100<>0))  //
    hand.stop_v100:=NOW;
    hand.res_v100:=hand.res_v100 + ((NOW-hand.start_v100) *86400);
    //hand.flag_v100:=1;
  end;
end;

local cip, cmd, ParL, StatL;
if (MESSAGEDLG('
?')=1)
  cmd := 'cip1';
  cip := &&cmd;
  cmd := 'ParL1';
  ParL := &&cmd;
  cmd := 'PStat1';
  StatL := &&cmd;
  if (ParL.P11=0) //P11-
    qout('
!!!');
    return();
  end;
  if ((cip.state=0) and (ParL.P11<>0)) //P11-
    StatL.start_time:=1;
  end;  
  cip.cmd:=3;
end;

function extern InitBlock;
  NewGlobalTime:=42700;
  KodArr1:=['','','','','',''];
  KodArr2:=['','','','','',''];
  KodArr3:=['','','','','','','','','','',''];
  KodArr4:=['','','','',''];
  KodArr5:=['','','','','','','','','','',''];//add
  KodArr6:=['','','','','','','',''];     //add
  nline:=1;
endf;
function extern GetWashStart(Stat);
  Return(Stat.Wash_Start/1000+NewGlobalTime);
endf;
function extern SetWashStart(Stat, NewTime);
  Stat.Wash_Start:=(NewTime-NewGlobalTime)*1000;
endf;
function extern DrawHLA_MSA(HLA);  //HLA
  if (HLA.allocate='C')
    
    if (HLA.M = 1)
       if (HLA.L_RED=1)
        HLA.setframes(4,0,2);
        HLA.selframe(1);
      end;
      if (HLA.L_SIREN=1)
        HLA.setframes(4,0,3);
        HLA.selframe(1);
      end;
      if ((HLA.L_RED=0) and (HLA.L_SIREN=0))
        HLA.setframes(4,0,1);
      end;
    else
      if (HLA.L_RED=1)
        HLA.setframes(0,2);
        HLA.selframe(1);
      end;
      if (HLA.L_SIREN=1)
        HLA.setframes(0,3);
        HLA.selframe(1);
      end;
      {
      if ((HLA.L_SIREN=1) and (HLA.L_RED=1))
        HLA.setframes(4,5);
        HLA.selframe(1);
      end;
      }
      if ((HLA.L_RED=0) and (HLA.L_SIREN=0))
        HLA.setframes(0);
        HLA.selframe(1);
      end;
    end;
  end;
endf;
function extern OnNRet( self );
  local nret;
  local n1000;
  local c1000;
  local ParL, cmd;
  //debug;
  if (self.line = prj.nline)
    cmd := 'ParL' + str(prj.nline,1);
    ParL := &&cmd;
    
    n1000 := self.r('RN1000');
    c1000 := self.r('rc1000');
    nret := ParL.P13; //
    
    if ( ( ParL.P45{
} <> 0 ) or ( ParL.P46{
} <> 0 ) )
      n1000.visible := True;
      c1000.visible := True;
      n1000.changeunit('N_Ret'+str(prj.nline,1));
      if ( ParL.P45{
} <> 0 )
        c1000.setnewtext( 'N' + LTrim(Str(ParL.P45)) );
      end;
      if ( ParL.P46{
} <> 0 )
        c1000.setnewtext( 'UPR' + LTrim(Str(ParL.P46)) );
      end;
    else
      n1000.visible := False;
      c1000.visible := False;
    end;
    
    
  end;
endf;
function extern Menu( Cip, MenuType );
  local MenuElementsCnt;
  local EnabledElements;
  local MenuIndex;
  local cmd;
  local res;
  local RecSubString;
  local RecString;
  local DevideRecPosition;
  local DevideRecNamePosition;
  local RecNmr;
  local RecName;
  local ArrayElementCnt;
  local promt;
  local ParL;
  // debug;
  if ( MenuType = 0 ) // 0-
    ArrayElementCnt := 27;
    RecString := Cip.Rec_List;
  else                // 1-
    ArrayElementCnt := 15;
    RecString := Cip.Prg_List;
  end;
  EnabledElements := array(2, ArrayElementCnt);
  if ( Len( RecString ) > 0 )
    MenuIndex := 0;
    repeat
      DevideRecPosition := AT( '||', RecString );
      if ( DevideRecPosition > 0 )
        RecSubString := RecString[1, DevideRecPosition - 1];
        RecString := RecString[ DevideRecPosition + 2];
        DevideRecNamePosition := AT( '##', RecSubString );
        RecNmr := RecSubString[ 1, DevideRecNamePosition - 1 ];
        RecName := RecSubString[ DevideRecNamePosition + 2 ];
        enabledElements[0, MenuIndex] := RecName;
        enabledElements[1, MenuIndex] := RecNmr;
        MenuIndex := MenuIndex + 1;
      else
        //        RecSubString := RecString;
        //        DevideRecNamePosition := AT( '##', RecSubString );
        //        RecNmr := RecSubString[ 1, DevideRecNamePosition - 1 ];
        //        RecName := RecSubString[ DevideRecNamePosition + 2 ];
        //        enabledElements[0, MenuIndex] := RecName;
        //        enabledElements[1, MenuIndex] := RecNmr;
        //        MenuIndex := MenuIndex + 1;
        menuElementsCnt := MenuIndex;
        break;
      end;
    until (False);
    
    MenuIndex := 0;
    
    cmd := '';
    while ( MenuIndex < menuElementsCnt )
      cmd := cmd + ',"' + enabledElements[0, MenuIndex] + '"';
      MenuIndex := MenuIndex + 1;
    end;
    cmd := cmd[2]; //
    
    cmd := 'ShowMenu(' + cmd + ')';
    res := &&cmd;
    
    if (res <> -1)
      //  debug;
      cmd := 'ParL' + Str( Cip.line, 1 );
      ParL := &&cmd;
      
      promt := enabledElements[0, res] + '?';
      
      if (MESSAGEDLG(promt)=1)
        if ( MenuType = 0 )
          ParL.P14 := enabledElements[1, res];
        else
          ParL.P15 := enabledElements[1, res];
        end;
      end;
    end;
    
  else
    QOut('
.');
  end;
endf;
function extern Klapan2Click(self);
  local promtOn;
  local promtOff;
  local objID;
  local cmd, PStat;
  objID := self.name;
  local CIP, hand;
  cmd := 'CIP' + Str( nline, 1 );
  CIP := &&cmd;
  cmd := 'hand_on';
  hand := &&cmd;
  if ( self.name[6,1] = 'V'{
    promtOn := '
    promtOff := '
  end;
  promtOn := promtOn + self.name + ' ?';
  promtOff := promtOff + self.name + ' ?';
  if ( self.State = 0 )
    if ( MESSAGEDLG( promtOff ) = 1 )
      self.State:=1;
      if (CIP.state=0)
        
        if (self.name[5,1] ='1')
          hand.str_l1:=hand.str_l1 + self.name + ',';
        end;
        
        if (self.name[5,1]='2')
          hand.str_l2:=hand.str_l2 + self.name + ',';
        end;
        
        if (self.name[5,1]='3')
          hand.str_l3:=hand.str_l3 + self.name + ',';
        end;
        
        if (self.name[5,1]='4')
          hand.str_l4:=hand.str_l4 + self.name + ',';
        end;
        
        if (self.name[5,1]='5')
          hand.str_l5:=hand.str_l5 + self.name + ',';
        end;
        
        if (self.name[5,1]='6')
          hand.str_l6:=hand.str_l6 + self.name + ',';
        end;
      else
        //debug;
        if (self.name[5,1] ='1')
          hand.str_w_l1:=hand.str_w_l1 + self.name + ',';
        end;
        
        if (self.name[5,1]='2')
          hand.str_w_l2:=hand.str_w_l2 + self.name + ',';
        end;
        
        if (self.name[5,1]='3')
          hand.str_w_l3:=hand.str_w_l3 + self.name + ',';
        end;
        
        if (self.name[5,1]='4')
          hand.str_w_l4:=hand.str_w_l4 + self.name + ',';
        end;
        
        if (self.name[5,1]='5')
          hand.str_w_l5:=hand.str_w_l5 + self.name + ',';
        end;
        
        if (self.name[5,1]='6')
          hand.str_w_l6:=hand.str_w_l6 + self.name + ',';
        end;
        
      end;
    end;
  else
    if ( MESSAGEDLG( promtOn ) = 1 )
      self.State:=0;
    end;
  end;
endf;

function extern NasosClick(self);
  local promtOn;
  local promtOff;
  local objID;
  local cmd, PStat;
  cmd := 'PStat' + Str( nline, 1 );
  PStat := &&cmd;
  objID := self.name;
  local CIP, hand, Pm;
  cmd := 'CIP' + Str( nline, 1 );
  CIP := &&cmd;
  cmd := 'hand_on';
  hand := &&cmd;
  cmd := 'PMain';
  Pm := &&cmd;
  local i;
  i := ShowMenu('
', '
', '
  if (i = 0)
    if (MESSAGEDLG('
 ' + self.Name + '?')=1)
      self.State := 1;
      //-----------------------------
      if ((self.name='M1') or (self.name='M2'))
        
        if (self.name='M1')
          hand.start_n1:=NOW;
        end;
        
        if (self.name='M2')
          hand.start_n2:=NOW;
        end;
        
      else
        
        if (CIP.state=0)
          if (self.name[5,1] ='1')
            hand.str_l1:=hand.str_l1 + self.name + ',';
          end;
          if (self.name[5,1]='2')
            hand.str_l2:=hand.str_l2 + self.name + ',';
          end;
          if (self.name[5,1] ='3')
            hand.str_l3:=hand.str_l3 + self.name + ',';
          end;
          if (self.name[5,1]='4')
            hand.str_l4:=hand.str_l4 + self.name + ',';
          end;
          if (self.name[5,1] ='5')
            hand.str_l5:=hand.str_l5 + self.name + ',';
          end;
          if (self.name[5,1]='6')
            hand.str_l6:=hand.str_l6 + self.name + ',';
          end;
        else
          //debug;
          if (self.name[5,1] ='1')
            hand.str_w_l1:=hand.str_w_l1 + self.name + ',';
          end;
          if (self.name[5,1]='2')
            hand.str_w_l2:=hand.str_w_l2 + self.name + ',';
          end;
          if (self.name[5,1] ='3')
            hand.str_w_l3:=hand.str_w_l3 + self.name + ',';
          end;
          if (self.name[5,1]='4')
            hand.str_w_l4:=hand.str_w_l4 + self.name + ',';
          end;
          if (self.name[5,1] ='5')
            hand.str_w_l5:=hand.str_w_l5 + self.name + ',';
          end;
          if (self.name[5,1]='6')
            hand.str_w_l6:=hand.str_w_l6 + self.name + ',';
          end;
        end;
      end;
      
      //-------------------------------
    end;
  end;
  if (i = 1)
    if (MESSAGEDLG('
 ' + self.Name + '?')=1)
      self.State := 0;
      //----------------------
      
      if ((self.name='M1') or (self.name='M2'))
        if (self.name='M1')
          hand.stop_n1:=NOW;
          hand.res_n1:=hand.res_n1 + ((NOW-hand.start_n1) *24* Pm.P28);
          hand.flag_n1:=1;
        end;
        
        if (self.name='M2')
          hand.stop_n2:=NOW;
          hand.res_n2:=hand.res_n2 + ((NOW-hand.start_n2) *24* Pm.P29);
          hand.flag_n2:=1;
        end;
      end;
      
      //--------------------
      
    end;
  end;
  if (i = 2) self.ShowDeviceInspector(); end;
endf;

function extern ChangeParam (self,par);
  local ret;
  ret:=par;
  if (self.priority>=7)
    if (MESSAGEDLG ('
?') = 1)
      ret:=EditF('
:', par);
    end;
  else
    qout('
!!!');
  end;
  return(ret);
endf;

function extern Hand_log_n1(self);
  local x, cmd, hand;
  {x:=null;
  x:=VarArrayCreate([0, 20], varVariant);
  x[0]  := 'table';
  x[1]  := 'reports.msa_modes';
  x[2]  := str(self.mca, 1) +' 0 '+ str( self.start_n1, 1);  //kod_id
  x[3]  := '
-' + str(self.mca, 1)+' 
';           // 
  x[4]  := 0;         //
  x[5]  := 'M1';
  x[6]  := ' ';
  x[7]  := 'datetype';
  x[8]  := self.start_n1;
  x[9]  := 'datetype';
  x[10]  := self.stop_n1;
  x[11]  := 0;                //
  x[12] := self.res_n1;                //
  x[13] := 0;
  x[14] := 0;
  x[15] := 0;
  x[16] := 0;
  x[17] := 0;
  x[18] := 0;
  x[19] := 0;
  x[20] := ' ';
  self.WriteToLog(11, x);
  x:=null;}
  self.res_n1:=0;
  self.start_n1:=0;
  self.stop_n1:=0;
endf;
function extern Hand_log_n2(self);
  local x, cmd, hand;
  {x:=null;
  x:=VarArrayCreate([0, 20], varVariant);
  x[0]  := 'table';
  x[1]  := 'reports.msa_modes';
  x[2]  := str(self.mca, 1) +' 0 '+ str( self.start_n2, 1);  //kod_id

  x[9]  := 'datetype';

  x[16] := 0;
  x[17] := 0;
  x[18] := 0;
  x[19] := 0;
  x[20] := ' ';
  self.WriteToLog(11, x);
  x:=null;}
  self.res_n2:=0;
  self.start_n2:=0;
  self.stop_n2:=0;
endf;
function extern Valves_draw(self, V_str);
  local V, cmd;
  cmd:=V_str;
  V:=&&cmd;
  if (self.allocate = 'C')
    if (V.State=1)
      V.setframes(0);
      V.selframe(2);
    end;
    
    if (V.State=0)
      V.setframes(0);
      V.selframe(1);
    end;
    
    if (V.State=-1)
      V.setframes(0);
      V.selframe(3);
    end;
    
    if (V.State=10)
      V.setframes(0, 6);
      V.selframe(1);
    end;
    
    if (V.State=11)
      V.setframes(0, 6);
      V.selframe(2);
    end;
    
    if (V.State=-2)
      V.setframes(0, 7);
      V.selframe(8);
    end;
    
    if (V.State=-3)
      V.setframes(0);
      V.selframe(3);
    end;
    
    if (V.State=21)
      V.setframes(4,0);
      V.selframe(2);
    end;
    
    if (V.State=20)
      V.setframes(4,0);
      V.selframe(1);
    end;
    
    {if (V.State=-11)
    V.setframes(4,0);
    V.selframe(3);
    end;                }
    
    if (V.State=30)
      V.setframes(4, 0, 6);
      V.selframe(1);
    end;
    
    if (V.State=31)
      V.setframes(4, 0, 6);
      V.selframe(2);
    end;
    
    if (V.State=-12)
      V.setframes(4, 0, 7);
      V.selframe(8);
    end;
    
    if (V.State=-13)
      V.setframes(4, 0);
      V.selframe(3);
    end;
  end;
endf;
function extern Pomp_draw(self, P_str);
  local P, cmd;
  cmd:=P_str;
  P:=&&cmd;
  if (P.allocate='C')
    
    if (P.State=-2)
      P.selframe(6);
    end;
    
    if (P.State=-1)
      P.selframe(5);
    end;
    
    if (P.State=1)
      P.animate:=true;
    else
      P.animate:=false;
    end;
    
    if (P.State=0)
      P.selframe(1);
    end;
  end;
endf;
function extern CTRDrop(self);
  if (MESSAGEDLG('
 ' + self.Name + '?')=1)
    Self.Value := 0;
  end;
endf;
